Applying the cut barely moves fidelity, only costs a downstream classifier about $0.07$ to $0.15$ AUC on average, and adding privacy guarantees don't make the data less fair.

</details>


### [199] [Language-encoded network topology enables large language models to reason about complex networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03229)
*Ucchwas Talukder Utsha,Sakib Mostafa,James Zou,Md Tauhidul Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BioGlyph将网络拓扑结构编译为可解释、可迁移的结构角色语言，使大语言模型（LLM）无需修改即可推理网络结构问题。在五个领域的二十个网络上，该方法相比基于边、数值和学习到的表示，在系统准确率上最高提升26个百分点，并在芽殖酵母蛋白互作网络中揭示了生物学组织规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM在自然语言任务上表现优异，但当网络以边列表、句子或测量表形式给出时，LLM难以进行结构推理，因为需要推断网络结构的语义含义。现有表示方法（基于边的、数值的、学习的）都无法让LLM有效捕捉节点在网络中的结构角色。

Method: BioGlyph结合图划分与结构测量来识别节点角色（如枢纽节点、社区核心、跨社区连接器），并使用固定规则将这些角色翻译为通用词汇。该表示通过结构角色、支持证据和语义后果来描述每个元素，网络和LLM本身均无需改变。

Result: 在跨越五个领域的二十个网络上，BioGlyph相比基于边、数值和学习得到的表示，将开放LLM的结构推理准确率最高提升26个百分点。消融实验显示提升来源于显式编码语义可解释的结构角色。增益在稠密、社区结构明显的网络中更为显著；在酵母蛋白互作网络中，跨社区连接器富集必需基因，而外围蛋白则出现缺失。

Conclusion: BioGlyph为语言模型和科学家提供了一种可解释的网络结构表示方法，能够弥合网络拓扑分析与自然语言推理之间的鸿沟，在生物学等交叉领域具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Networks describe systems in biology and beyond, from protein interactions and social relationships to power grids and citation records. Reasoning about such systems requires understanding their structure: which elements are central, which connections bridge separate communities, and how it changes when elements are removed. Although large language models (LLMs) excel at natural language, they struggle with such questions when networks are given as edge lists, sentences or measurement tables, because their structural meaning must be inferred. Here we introduce BioGlyph, which compiles network topology into an interpretable and transferable language of structural roles. BioGlyph combines graph partitioning and structural measurements to identify roles such as hubs, community cores and cross-community connectors, and fixed rules to translate them into a universal vocabulary. The representation describes each element through its structural role, supporting evidence and semantic consequences, leaving both the network and the LLM unchanged. Across twenty networks spanning five domains, BioGlyph substantially improves open LLMs' ability to answer structural reasoning questions, outperforming edge-based, numerical and learned representations by up to 26 percentage points in system accuracy. Ablations show that the gain comes from explicitly encoding structural roles in semantically interpretable terms. The gain is more prominent in dense, community-structured networks and diminishes in sparse networks whose topology is more readily inferred from text. In a budding-yeast protein-interaction network, BioGlyph exposes biological organization: cross-community connectors are enriched for essential genes, whereas peripheral proteins are depleted. BioGlyph thus provides an interpretable representation for both language models and scientists to reason about network structure.

</details>


### [200] [The 2026 PNPL Competition: Word Classification and Efficient Cross-Subject Generalisation in LibriBrain100](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03231)
*Francesco Mantegna,Gereon Elvers,Dulhan Jayalath,Gilad Landau,Tasha Kim,Miran Özdogan,Luisa Kurth,Teyun Kwon,SungJun Cho,Benjamin Ballyk,Alex Fung,Anna Greer,Pratik Somaiya,Christian Herff,Yorguin Mantilla Ramos,Hamza Abdelhedi,Karim Jerbi,Greg Farquhar,Brendan Shillingford,Mark Woolrich,Oiwi Parker Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了2026年PNPL竞赛及其LibriBrain100数据集，该竞赛旨在通过单词分类任务推进非侵入式语音解码研究，设置了Deep track（主题内分类）和Broad track（跨主题泛化）两个互补赛道，后者将微调数据量从40分钟逐步缩减至10分钟，向临床可行的非侵入式脑机接口迈进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2025年PNPL竞赛虽然在语音检测和音素分类任务上取得了显著成果（F1-macro分别达95.6%和73.6%），但依赖的是单一受试者约50小时的MEG数据。实用脑机接口需要从几分钟的数据泛化到新用户，现有方法无法满足这一需求。

Method: 2026年PNPL竞赛扩展了LibriBrain数据集为LibriBrain100，新增32个受试者（每人约40分钟）并将单受试者数据扩充至约80小时。竞赛推进课程至单词分类任务，设置两个互补赛道：Deep track聚焦于大规模主题内单词分类以追求最佳性能；Broad track聚焦于跨主题泛化，逐步减少主题特定微调数据从40到20再到10分钟。

Result: 竞争设计采用双轨制，Deep track力争最佳主题内单词分类性能，Broad track系统评估跨受试者泛化能力并在临床可行时间范围内（10分钟微调数据）测试解码效果。

Conclusion: 通过逐年推进竞赛课程并引入多受试者数据集，2026年PNPL竞赛旨在验证从少量快速采集数据中实现跨受试者语音解码的可行性，将非侵入式脑机接口推向能够恢复严重瘫痪患者沟通能力的临床应用。

Abstract: The ambition of the 2025 PNPL competition (Landau et al., 2025) was to launch a multi-year curriculum for non-invasive speech decoding. Designed to progress from foundational tasks toward the linguistic complexity required for a practical brain-computer interface (BCI), it set the stage with speech detection and phoneme classification tasks. Winning submissions reached F1-macro scores of 95.6% and 73.6% on the respective tasks (Elvers et al., 2026), highly significant advances. This success was built on the LibriBrain dataset (Özdogan et al., 2025), the largest within-subject MEG dataset recorded at the time with ${\sim}50$ hours of data for one subject. However, while within-subject scale drives strong decoding performance, a practical BCI must generalise to new users from minutes of data, not hours.
  The 2026 PNPL competition responds to this challenge with LibriBrain100 (Mantegna et al., 2026), an extended LibriBrain dataset with 32 additional subjects (${\sim}40$ minutes each) plus even more within-subject data (${\sim}80$ hours). Advancing the curriculum of tasks to focus on word classification, two complementary tracks are presented in this competition: the Deep track targets within-subject word classification at scale, aiming at the best possible performance; the Broad track targets cross-subject generalisation, progressively reducing the amount of subject-specific fine-tuning data from ${\sim}40$ to ${\sim}20$ to ${\sim}10$ minutes, a duration that falls within a clinically feasible range and brings us a step closer to a non-invasive BCI capable of restoring communication to people living with profound paralysis.

</details>


### [201] [B2B Customer Conversion Prediction: A Document Representation, Graph Theory, and CatBoost Driven Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03239)
*Tianqi Wang,Sheikh Shams Azam,Wan Eih Huang,Anton Wiranata,Christopher G. Brinton,Jan P. Allebach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究B2B一次性销售场景下的客户转化预测问题，提出基于多键聚合联系人至客户级别、利用新型聚类架构处理公司名拼写差异，生成特征后使用CatBoost模型实现91%的预测准确率，并据此提供个性化活动推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在B2B一次性销售情境中，购买周期可长达数月甚至数年，精准识别高潜力客户并推荐个性化营销活动对有效营销至关重要。因此需要对客户数据进行聚合、特征生成及转化预测。

Method: 提出了一种基于多键将个体联系人聚合到B2B客户级别的算法；针对公司名称等非标准化键，设计了一种能处理拼写错误和拼写变体等不规则情况的新型聚类架构；随后定义并生成一组特征，应用CatBoost模型进行客户转化预测。

Result: 该框架达到了91%的预测准确率。

Conclusion: 基于预测结果和模型分析，讨论了如何推荐个性化活动以促进转化，从而实现更有效的营销。

Abstract: In the one-time selling B2B context, the buying cycle may last months or even years. During the long process, targeting customers that have a high potential to make purchases and recommending personalized campaigns accordingly are important for effective marketing. For this goal, we study the following problems, B2B customer data aggregation, customer feature generation, and prediction of whether a B2B customer would show interest in making a purchase (i.e., prediction of conversion into sales funnel). We propose an algorithm to aggregate individual contacts to the B2B customer level based on multiple keys. For non-standardized keys such as company names, we propose a novel architecture to cluster them in a domain encompassing irregularities such as spelling mistakes and spelling variants. We then define and generate a set of features and apply the CatBoost model for customer conversion prediction. Our framework achieves 91\% prediction accuracy. Based on the prediction results and analysis of the model, we then discuss personalized campaign recommendations to foster conversion.

</details>


### [202] [FlowBalance: Verifier-Grounded Self-Improvement from On-Policy Reasoning Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03241)
*Zixun Huang,Kishan Panaganti,Haitao Mi,Leowei Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowBalance是一种基于验证器的自改进方法，通过将策略自身的token级对数概率增益与验证器派生的组优势进行校准，在轨迹平衡框架下学习完整响应的归一化分布，从而在不使用独立token级模仿损失的情况下实现结果校准的自我引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型可以从自身on-policy经验中改进，但这一内部循环是脆弱的：终端验证器提供可靠但稀疏的监督，而密集的相同模型引导可能强化错误的自信或将学习过度集中于狭窄的解决方案模式。

Method: FlowBalance为每个on-policy轨迹利用冻结的训练时策略视图（利用特权上下文）产生token级对数概率增益，聚合为轨迹级自我引导分数。该分数与验证器派生的组优势进行校准：在正优势轨迹上保留引导，在负优势轨迹上反转引导，当rollout组无结果偏好时禁用引导。生成的能量对参考策略进行指数重加权，剖面轨迹平衡通过每个rollout组的单次对数分配估计拟合归一化目标，实现基于轨迹平衡的结果校准自我引导，无需单独token级模仿损失。

Result: 在数学推理任务上，FlowBalance在Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B上均优于FlowRL的平均性能，同时提高了训练速度和稳定性，避免了OPSD的响应长度坍缩问题，并在AIME24受控诊断中展现出更高的正确策略多样性。

Conclusion: 论文建立了组内对比保持、最小变化反向KL表征、验证器对目标奖励的单调控制和针对被拒绝响应的假阳性自我引导的精确修正等理论保证，表明该方法是稳健且可证明有效的自改进框架。

Abstract: A reasoning model can improve from its own on-policy experience, but this inner loop is fragile: terminal verifiers provide reliable yet sparse supervision, while dense same-model guidance can reinforce false confidence or overconcentrate learning on a narrow solution mode. We introduce FlowBalance, a verifier-grounded self-improvement method that learns a normalized distribution over complete responses. For each on-policy trajectory, a frozen training-time view of the same policy uses privileged context to produce token-level log-probability gains, which are aggregated into a trajectory-level self-guidance score. FlowBalance calibrates this score with the verifier-derived group advantage: guidance is retained on positive-advantage trajectories, reversed on negative-advantage trajectories, and disabled when the rollout group provides no outcome preference. The resulting energy exponentially reweights a reference policy, and profiled trajectory balance fits the normalized target with one log-partition estimate per rollout group. This realizes outcome-calibrated self-guidance via trajectory balance, without a separate token-level imitation loss. Our analysis establishes within-group contrast preservation, a minimum-change reverse-KL characterization, monotonic verifier control of target reward, and an exact correction against false-positive self-guidance on rejected responses. On mathematical reasoning, FlowBalance improves average performance over FlowRL on both Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B, while also improving training speed and stability, avoiding direct OPSD's response-length collapse, and exhibiting higher correct-strategy diversity in a controlled AIME24 diagnostic.

</details>


### [203] [Selective Hypergraph Refinement for Frozen Graph Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03265)
*Zimo Si*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对"冻结图聚类"的后处理问题，提出选择性超图细化（SHR）方法：在不更新模型参数、节点表示和原始图结构的前提下，利用属性超图补充高阶关系，对已训练并冻结的聚类模型的簇分配进行选择性修正。实验表明该方法在多数场景下带来小幅但显著的宏F1提升，簇变更比例极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图聚类方法通过优化模型参数和节点表示来提升聚类性能，但对于已经训练好且冻结的模型，缺乏有效的进一步提升手段。论文探索一个被忽视的问题：在模型冻结后，是否仍存在可利用的聚类优化空间。

Method: SHR方法的核心思路是：1) 利用属性超图表达普通图难以捕获的高阶关系；2) 基于该超图生成候选残差方向；3) 使用图结构、节点属性和匹配空模型（matched-null）证据来评估每个候选修正的可靠性；4) 仅对具有充分支撑的节点进行簇更新，对其余节点保留原分配。理论分析揭示节点是否改变簇由本征分配间隙和修正的方向强度共同决定。

Result: 在受控公共测试集上，15个骨干网络-数据集组合中有13个产生正的宏平均增益，1个无变化，1个为负。细胞级宏增益为0.066个百分点（95%自助法置信区间[0.030, 0.107]），平均仅有0.209%的硬分配发生变化。在更广泛的15组合原生接口评估中，宏增益为0.137个百分点，平均变更比例为0.375%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，冻结聚类输出在训练后仍保留有限但可测量的细化空间。效应在骨干网络-数据集对之间具有异质性，且更广泛的覆盖会增加负迁移暴露风险。

Abstract: Existing graph-clustering methods typically improve clustering performance by optimizing model parameters and node representations. Effective means of further improving the clustering results of an already trained and frozen model, however, remain limited. We study post-processing for frozen graph clustering. After checkpoint fixation, the procedure uses no labels and updates neither model parameters, node representations, nor the original graph structure. Instead, it exploits an attribute hypergraph to supplement higher-order relations that ordinary graphs cannot readily express, thereby refining existing cluster assignments. Because global hypergraph refinement can yield both performance gains and erroneous updates, we propose Selective Hypergraph Refinement (SHR). The method generates candidate residual directions from the hypergraph and evaluates their reliability using graph structure, node attributes, and matched-null evidence. It updates only nodes with sufficient support and otherwise retains their original assignments. Further analysis shows that whether a node changes cluster is jointly governed by its native assignment gap and the directional strength of the refinement. In a controlled common-suite evaluation, 13 of 15 backbone-dataset cells had a positive mean macro gain, one produced exact no-action, and one was negative. The cell-equal macro gain was 0.066 pp (95% bootstrap CI, [0.030, 0.107] pp), while only 0.209% of hard assignments changed on average. A broader 15-combination native-interface evaluation yielded a macro gain of 0.137 pp at a mean change ratio of 0.375%. These results indicate that frozen clustering outputs retain a limited but measurable refinement space after training. The effect is heterogeneous across backbone-dataset pairs, and broader coverage also increases exposure to negative transfer.

</details>


### [204] [Latent Energy Action Planning with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03294)
*Phu Pham,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LEAP（潜在能量动作规划）框架，将完整动作序列作为可微变量，通过冻结的LeWorldModel（LeWM）进行优化。LEAP将终端潜在状态目标匹配与终端窗口状态能量结合，解决单一潜在目标在模型预测控制中的描述符不匹配问题。在四个控制域上，LEAP将LeWM+CEM的成功率从77.5%提升至94.8%，提升17.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型支持从高维观测中进行高效模型预测控制，但优化单一学习到的潜在目标时，会倾向于选择解码器预测的终端描述符与目标描述符不匹配的动作序列，导致控制失败。需要一种能同时约束潜在空间匹配和描述符级匹配的规划方法。

Method: LEAP将完整动作视界（action horizon）视为可微变量，通过冻结的LeWM进行优化。其核心包含三重机制：(1) 终端潜在状态与目标潜在状态匹配；(2) 终端窗口状态能量约束，要求解码器预测的终端描述符与目标描述符一致；(3) 冻结的目标条件提议（proposal）初始化搜索，拟牛顿求解器通过自回归展开精化动作，优化后投影强制动作落在允许范围内。

Result: 在四个控制域上使用官方发布的LeWM检查点，在与LeWM+CEM匹配的协议下，LEAP将平均成功率从77.5%提升到94.8%，提升17.3个百分点，同时保持LeWM表示和预测器冻结不变。

Conclusion: 通过将终端级潜在能量与描述符级能量耦合，LEAP在无需重新训练世界模型的前提下显著提升了潜在世界模型的模型预测控制性能，验证了在冻结模型条件下通过高阶优化与能量匹配可获得实质性性能增益。

Abstract: Latent world models support efficient model predictive control from high-dimensional observations, yet optimizing a single learned latent objective can favor action sequences whose decoder-predicted terminal descriptor does not match the goal descriptor. We introduce Latent Energy Action Planning (LEAP), which treats the complete action horizon as a differentiable variable and optimizes it through a frozen LeWorldModel (LeWM). LEAP couples terminal latent goal matching with a terminal-window state energy. Low energy requires the predicted terminal latent to agree with the goal latent and the decoder-predicted terminal descriptor to agree with the goal descriptor. A frozen goal-conditioned proposal initializes the search, a quasi-Newton solver refines actions through the autoregressive rollout, and post-optimization projection enforces the admissible action range. Across four control domains using the officially released LeWM checkpoints, the complete LEAP planning system raises mean success from 77.5% for LeWM planned with the cross-entropy method (LeWM+CEM) to 94.8% under a matched protocol, a 17.3-percentage-point improvement, while retaining the frozen LeWM representation and predictor.

</details>


### [205] [Geometry-Aware Graph Construction via Adaptive Spectral Bandwidth Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03306)
*Ecem Bozkurt,Antonio Ortega*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应高斯核带宽选择方法，使核的谱复杂度与底层流形的内在复杂度一致。该方法通过逐节点匹配核的有效秩与基于最小生成树估计的局部内在维度，实现了带宽的自动选择，并在CIFAR-100上对六个SSL编码器验证了其优于固定带宽及现有自适应方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于高斯核的图方法（如谱聚类、扩散映射、稀疏核回归图）高度依赖带宽σ的选择。σ过小会高估局部复杂度导致样本被过度区分，σ过大会导致方向塌缩、条件数发散且丧失几何判别能力。现有方法缺乏原则性的带宽选择策略。

Method: 提出使核的谱复杂度与底层流形内在复杂度一致的带宽选择原理。具体实现为逐节点带宽准则：通过与最小生成树（MST）估计的局部内在维度联合匹配核的有效秩，将搜索锚定在流形一致的对数-对数尺度区间内。

Result: 在CIFAR-100数据集上使用六个自监督学习（SSL）编码器进行实验，自适应带宽方法在留一法（LOO）分类和标签传播（LP）准确率上一致优于固定带宽方法和现有竞争性自适应方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的谱复杂度与流形复杂度匹配的带宽选择原理及逐节点实现方案，能有效提升基于核的图方法在下游任务中的性能，验证了方法在不同SSL特征上的泛化能力。

Abstract: Kernelized graph methods - spectral clustering, diffusion maps, and sparse kernel -regression graphs - that use Gaussian kernels depend on the choice of Gaussian bandwidth sigma, which governs the spectral character of the local kernel operator. When sigma is too small, the kernel overestimates local complexity and treats each sample as an independent direction; when sigma is too large, the kernel collapses multiple directions together, the condition number diverges, and all geometric discrimination is lost. We propose a choice of scale to make the spectral complexity of the kernel consistent with the intrinsic complexity of the underlying manifold. We propose a per-node bandwidth criterion that operationalizes this principle by jointly matching the kernel's effective rank to the local intrinsic dimension estimated via minimum spanning tree, anchoring the search in the manifold-consistent log-log scaling regime. We evaluate SSL embeddings from six encoders on CIFAR-100, showing that adaptive bandwidth consistently improves leave-one-out (LOO) classification and label propagation (LP) accuracy over fixed-bandwidth methods and competing adaptive methods.

</details>


### [206] [Risk and Anomaly Identification for Distribution Network Optimal Operation Based on Reinforcement Learning and Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03308)
*Ziqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种显式不确定性感知的深度强化学习框架，用于配电网络中的联合风险与异常识别问题，通过分布性和贝叶斯深度强化学习实现二阶不确定性量化，将总不确定性分解为偶然不确定性和认知不确定性，分别用于表征固有运行风险和分布外异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代配电网络的可靠运行要求在普遍不确定性下及时发现运行风险和异常事件。实际中，运营商需要识别随机但分布内条件下的固有风险，以及分布外行为（如异常负荷模式、极端天气或网络物理攻击）对应的异常。传统方法难以同时处理这两类问题，因此需要一个显式不确定性感知的框架来联合解决风险与异常识别。

Method: 提出一个深度强化学习框架，集成分布性深度强化学习和贝叶斯深度强化学习，实现二阶不确定性量化方案。该方法将总不确定性分解为偶然不确定性（aleatoric）和认知不确定性（epistemic）两个分量：认知估计用于驱动训练期间的探索和部署期间的分布外检测及回退控制，而偶然估计用于表征固有运行风险。

Result: 仿真结果验证了所提出深度强化学习智能体的性能以及不确定性量化方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的不确定性感知深度强化学习框架能够有效联合识别配电网络中的固有运行风险和分布外异常，通过二阶不确定性分解实现更可靠的网络运行决策。

Abstract: Reliable operation of modern distribution networks requires timely identification of operational risks and anomalous events under pervasive uncertainty. In practice, operators must identify risks that are inherent in stochastic yet in-distribution conditions, and anomalies that correspond to out-of-distribution behaviors such as unusual load patterns, extreme weather or cyber-physical attacks. This paper addresses this joint risk and anomaly identification problem for optimal distribution network operation and proposes a deep reinforcement learning framework that is explicitly uncertainty aware. We integrate distributional and Bayesian deep reinforcement learning to realize a second- order uncertainty quantification scheme that decomposes total uncertainty into aleatoric and epistemic components, which are respectively used to characterize inherent risk and out-of- distribution anomalies. The resulting epistemic estimates drive both exploration during training and out-of-distribution detec- tion with fallback control during deployment, whereas aleatoric estimates are used to characterize intrinsic operational risk. Simulation results demonstrate the performance of our DRL agent and the effectiveness of the uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [207] [DE-Venus: A Data-Efficient RLVR Framework for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03324)
*Shenzhi Yang,Guangcheng Zhu,Kai Tang,Zhengqing Zang,Xing Zheng,Haobo Wang,Yingfan Ma,Bowen Song,Bo Han,Bo An,Lei Feng,Weiqiang Wang,Junbo Zhao,Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DE-Venus是一个面向数据高效的RLVR（带可验证奖励的强化学习）统一框架，将监督视为跨数据准备与策略优化的演进状态，支持七种代表性方法，在使用仅10%标签或13%相关数据的条件下保持或提升模型质量，并在部分业务配置中将收敛步数减少63%-75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR能提升大语言模型的推理能力，但其实际规模化受限于昂贵的on-policy采样成本以及大规模获取可靠监督目标的代价。现有方法分别解决样本选择、不完整监督或噪声标签问题，且常将监督逻辑与分布式训练耦合，阻碍了受控对比与复用。

Method: DE-Venus将监督生命周期组织为三个模块：主动数据选择（分配训练和标注预算）、弱监督构建（从未标注样本推导学习信号）、训练时监督精炼（过滤或纠正不可靠监督）。通过将方法特定的决策表达为离线数据集转换或对目标、奖励、batch和优势的在线变换，同时保留verl的分布式执行契约，支持七种代表性方法及一套数据选择管线。

Result: 在公开基准和三个业务场景中，分离配置仅使用10%的标签或13%的相关数据即可保持或提升模型质量；选定的业务配置还将观测到的收敛步数减少了63%-75%。

Conclusion: DE-Venus在保持可扩展RL执行能力的同时，有效降低了标注与训练成本，为该领域提供了一个解耦、可复用且可受控比较的统一框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves large language model reasoning, but its practical scaling is constrained by expensive on-policy rollouts and the cost of obtaining reliable targets at scale. Existing methods address sample selection, incomplete supervision, or noisy labels separately, often entangling supervision logic with distributed training and hindering controlled comparison and reuse. We present DE-Venus, a unified framework for data-efficient RLVR that treats supervision as evolving state across data preparation and policy optimization. It organizes this lifecycle into three modules: Active Data Selection allocates training and annotation budgets; Weak Supervision Construction derives learning signals from unlabeled examples; and Training-Time Supervision Refinement filters or corrects unreliable supervision. DE-Venus supports seven representative methods and a data-selection pipeline by expressing method-specific decisions as offline dataset transitions or online transformations of targets, rewards, batches, and advantages while preserving verl's distributed execution contracts. Across public benchmarks and three business scenarios, separate configurations preserve or improve model quality with only 10% of labels or as little as 13% of relevant data; selected business configurations also reduce observed convergence steps by 63%--75%. DE-Venus thus reduces annotation and training costs without sacrificing scalable RL execution.

</details>


### [208] [A Large Open Multi-Energy Corpus of Soil Compaction Tests, with Machine-Learning Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03337)
*Sompote Youwai,Chana Phutthananon,Warat Kongkitkul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文公布了一个包含2,854次实验室压实试验的大型数据库（来自六个公开来源、162个来源组、四种Proctor能量水平），并通过零气隙条件筛选剔除了约12%物理上不可能的记录。基于该语料库，作者提出利用分类套件和压实标准估计最优干密度与最优含水量，并比较了多种机器学习与符号回归方法的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的压实参数相关性研究通常局限于单一实验室、单一压实能量，样本量仅数百且很少公开发布。这导致工程填土的最优干密度和最优含水量预测缺乏可靠、广泛的数据基础，且大量已发表压实数据可能存在物理不可行的问题。

Method: 构建包含2,854条压实试验记录的开放语料库，覆盖六个来源、162个来源组和四种Proctor能量。对每条记录按其来源命名的Proctor方法进行审计，不推断能量等级。用零气隙条件进行数据筛选。在此基础上，通过表格基础模型（tabular foundation model）和符号回归，仅用一套分类指标和压实标准估计最优干密度与最优含水量，预测通过相态关系耦合，保证不超越零气隙线。

Result: 语料库最优饱和度中位数为0.815，变异系数11%。表格基础模型在随机划分下密度和含水量R²分别为0.824和0.784；按来源组划分时为0.727和0.696；整体留出某个来源时为0.520和0.614。压实能量在边际上影响微弱但在条件预测中起决定性作用：66条修正Proctor记录中，有能量变量时R²为0.740，无能量时为-0.651。

Conclusion: 已发布的压实数据中有相当比例（约12%）物理上不可能，需要筛选。压实参数的预测应被视为筛选工具而非验收依据。符号回归得到的闭式解通过相态关系保证了预测对不超出零气隙界限的物理合理性。

Abstract: Every engineered fill is specified by a maximum dry density and an optimum moisture content. Each determination needs a full Proctor test. Published correlations rest on one to four hundred specimens, usually from one laboratory at one compactive energy, and are seldom released. This paper releases a corpus without those limits. It holds 2,854 laboratory compaction tests from six public sources, across 162 provenance groups and four Proctor energy levels, with fines from 1.5 to 100%. Every record is audited to the Proctor method its source names, and no energy is inferred. Screening on the zero-air-voids condition removed 11.8% of harmonised records, and 5.7% of those with a measured specific gravity. A material share of published compaction data is physically impossible. The optimum degree of saturation over the corpus is 0.815 at a coefficient of variation of 11%. That is a baseline, not a constant. Both parameters are then estimated from one classification suite and the compaction standard. A tabular foundation model reaches R2 0.824 for density and 0.784 for water content under random folds. It reaches 0.727 and 0.696 with folds drawn around provenance, and 0.520 and 0.614 with a whole source held out. Compactive energy is negligible marginally yet decisive conditionally. Density on the 66 modified-Proctor records is predicted at R2 0.740 with it and -0.651 without. Symbolic regression yields closed forms coupled through a phase relation. No predicted pair can then exceed the zero-air-voids line. The predictions are for screening, not acceptance.

</details>


### [209] [Gradients Know What Outcomes Don't: Unlocking Reinforcement Learning for LLM Reasoning with Gradient-Aligned Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03342)
*Leqi Zheng,Jinbo Su,Fang Niu,Chaokun Wang,Weiping Wang,Jiajun Zhang,Shannan Yan,Jie Wu,Zhaolu Kang,Rong Fu,Hang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Gradient-Aligned Reward (GAR)，一种在策略自身的梯度空间中操作的新型稠密奖励方法。它利用截断的反向传播从输出投影层提取紧凑梯度向量，通过与专家锚点梯度的余弦相似度生成推理感知的稠密奖励，仅增加不到9%的墙钟开销。该方法在Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B上与GRPO等基线相比，在竞赛级数学基准上持续改进，并无需领域特定数据即可泛化到GPQA Diamond和MMLU-Pro。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可验证奖励RLVR方法使用二元奖励无法区分正确的轨迹；而现有稠密奖励替代方案要么忽略训练语料中已有的专家解，要么需要昂贵的离线标注。作者旨在利用已存在的专家解决方案，设计一种无需额外标注的高效稠密奖励机制。

Method: GAR在策略自身的梯度空间中操作：对每条rollout通过输出投影层进行截断反向传播来提取紧凑的梯度向量，然后计算该向量与专家锚点梯度的余弦相似度，作为稠密的推理感知奖励。作者证明了该余弦距离可分解为预测误差因子与激活模式因子的乘积，从而刻画了对齐信号的测量内容。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B上，GAR在竞赛级数学基准上持续优于GRPO及其他基线方法，并且无需领域特定数据即可迁移到GPQA Diamond和MMLU-Pro。额外开销仅为不到9%的墙钟时间。

Conclusion: GAR利用训练语料中已有的专家解作为锚点，在策略梯度空间中构造稠密奖励信号，既避免了昂贵的离线标注，又能提供比二元结果奖励更细粒度的推理反馈，且计算开销极低、泛化能力强。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) drives chain-of-thought reasoning in large language models, yet its binary outcome reward cannot distinguish among correct trajectories. Existing dense reward alternatives, from surface heuristics to process reward models, either ignore the expert solutions already present in training corpora or require expensive offline annotation. We propose Gradient-Aligned Reward (GAR), which operates in the policy's own gradient space: truncated backpropagation through the output projection layer extracts a compact gradient vector for each rollout, and cosine similarity with an expert-anchor gradient yields a dense, reasoning-aware reward with less than 9% wall-clock overhead. We prove that this cosine admits a multiplicative decomposition into prediction-error and activation-pattern factors, providing a concrete characterization of what the alignment signal measures. On Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B, GAR consistently improves over GRPO and other baselines on competition-level math benchmarks and transfers to GPQA Diamond and MMLU-Pro without domain-specific data. Code and data are available at https://github.com/LQgdwind/GAR.

</details>


### [210] [From Zero to Hero: An Open LLM Ecosystem for Armenian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03350)
*Erik Arakelyan,Khatun Avetisyan,Meri Davtyan,Heghine Grigoryan,Nane Khachatryan,Hayk Shahsuvaryan,Henrik Sergoyan,Vahan Martirosyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布了针对亚美尼亚语（形态丰富、低资源语言）的开放数据和模型：ArmWeb（437万亚美尼亚语新闻文档）和ArmSTEM（37.3万英-亚平行数学与科学题目及分步解答，经翻译与双重验证）。基于Gemma-4-E4B继续预训练得到arm-gemma-e4b，在开放亚美尼亚语模型中表现最优。消融实验发现仅新闻继续预训练提升流畅性但侵蚀知识，加入少量验证过的STEM翻译数据可逆转该损失。文中还揭示了大型公开亚美尼亚语语料库与web评估集存在严重重叠问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚美尼亚语作为形态丰富且低资源的语言，预训练数据稀缺，此前没有任何开放亚美尼亚语大语言模型同时发布完整的训练数据和复现配方，阻碍了该语言模型研究与社区发展。

Method: 1) 构建并验证ArmWeb（437万条亚美尼亚语新闻文档语料库）和ArmSTEM（37.3万条英-亚平行数学/科学题目，通过答案保持的LLM判断和人工评估双重验证翻译质量）；2) 在Gemma-4-E4B上进行继续预训练；3) 通过消融实验对比纯新闻继续预训练与混合STEM数据训练的效果；4) 分析现有公开语料库与评估集的词元重叠问题。

Result: arm-gemma-e4b在各项评测中超越所有现有开放亚美尼亚语模型及未适配的基座模型；消融显示新闻-only继续预训练提升流畅性但导致知识衰退（该模式在现有亚美尼亚语模型中也被观察到），而加入少量验证过的翻译STEM数据可逆转知识损失；同时发现最大公开亚美尼亚语语料库与web评估面板高度重叠，FineWeb-2内部存在训练/测试自我重叠。

Conclusion: 作者主张并实现了首个具有完全开放训练数据和复现配方的亚美尼亚语LLM，证明了高质量领域数据的相对比例对知识保持至关重要，同时指出了现有公共语料库污染评估集的问题，敦促社区关注数据来源与评估可信度。

Abstract: Pretraining data for Armenian, a morphologically rich and low-resource language, is scarce, and no open Armenian LLM has been released with the data and recipe needed to reproduce it. To address this gap, we curate and release two datasets. ArmWeb is an extensively validated corpus of 4.37M Armenian news documents. ArmSTEM is a parallel English-Armenian collection of 373K math and science problems with step-by-step solutions, translated into Armenian and verified through both answer-preserving LLM judgment and human evaluation. Continued pretraining of Gemma-4-E4B on these datasets yields arm-gemma-e4b, which outperforms every existing open Armenian model as well as its unadapted base, and is the first open Armenian LLM with complete training data and recipe. Our ablations show that news-only continued pretraining improves fluency while eroding knowledge, a pattern we also observe in existing Armenian models, and that a small share of verified translated STEM data reverses the loss. We further find that the largest public Armenian corpora overlap web-derived evaluation panels heavily, including a train/test self-overlap inside FineWeb-2. We openly release all data, models, and code.

</details>


### [211] [Time Without Timesteps: Simulating Coupled Dynamical Systems via Self-Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03358)
*Liyu Zerihun,Mark Shinyoung Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将耦合动力系统仿真从逐步时间推进重构为轨迹级不动点问题的方法：为每个子系统训练神经网络代理，将完整驱动轨迹和初值直接映射到完整输出轨迹，通过经典波形松弛思想强制执行轨迹间的自洽性。在耦合van der Pol振荡器和Hodgkin-Huxley神经元网络上，求解深度从参考积分器的1500步降低到4-10次牛顿迭代；梯度计算也不再依赖时间递归，而是通过GMRES求解线性系统，内存与求解深度无关。通过学习算子的谱半径可预先预测耦合求解的收敛性，在边界之外反向传播发散、Neumann伴随失效，而隐式梯度精度仍保持在0.04%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值仿真以因果方式逐时间步推进，导致仿真深度与时间步数成正比，梯度在时间上递归传播，内存和计算代价随轨迹长度线性增长。作者希望打破这一时间深度限制，将仿真转化为固定点问题，使顺序深度变为可迭代的求解步数（而非时间步数），从而大幅降低仿真与梯度计算的计算代价。

Method: 1) 对每个子系统训练神经网络代理，学习从完整驱动轨迹和初始条件到完整输出轨迹的映射；2) 采用波形松弛（waveform relaxation）框架，将耦合系统组装为在完整轨迹上强制自洽的固定点问题；3) 使用牛顿迭代求解固定点，并将梯度转化为线性系统，通过GMRES迭代求解，使内存需求与求解深度无关；4) 利用学习算子Jacobian的谱半径作为收敛性的前验预测指标。

Result: 在耦合van der Pol振荡器和Hodgkin-Huxley神经元网络上：求解仅需4-10次牛顿迭代（参考积分器需1500步）；隐式梯度在谱半径边界之外仍保持0.04%的精度，而反向传播发散、Neumann伴随失效；谱半径可预先预测耦合求解的收敛性。同时报告了代理误差退化场景下的失败模式。

Conclusion: 轨迹级固定点公式化是一种有效的替代逐步时间推进的仿真范式，能显著缩短顺序求解深度、降低梯度内存需求，并可通过谱半径进行收敛性先验预测。该方法的成功取决于神经网络代理的质量，代理误差是其主要局限。

Abstract: Numerical simulation of dynamical systems is usually organized as a causal march through time: each state is computed from the previous one. We explore a different formulation for coupled systems. For each subsystem type we train a neural surrogate mapping a full driving trajectory and initial condition directly to a full output trajectory; following classical waveform relaxation, coupled systems are assembled by enforcing self-consistency among these trajectories: simulation becomes a fixed-point problem over complete trajectories rather than a stepwise rollout. On coupled van der Pol oscillators and Hodgkin-Huxley neuron networks, sequential depth becomes the number of solver iterations: 4-10 Newton iterations where the reference integrator takes 1500 steps. The gradient likewise loses its time recursion: it becomes a linear system solved by GMRES at memory independent of solver depth. A single scalar measured from the learned operator, the spectral radius of its Jacobian, predicts in advance where the coupled solve will converge; past that boundary, unrolled backpropagation diverges and a Neumann adjoint fails, while the implicit gradient remains correct to 0.04%. We report where the approach succeeds and where surrogate error degrades it.

</details>


### [212] [SimpleDesign: A Joint Model for Protein Sequence and Structure Codesign](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03377)
*Jiarui Lu,Yuyang Wang,Yizhe Zhang,Jiatao Gu,Navdeep Jaitly,Joshua M. Susskind,Miguel Ángel Bautista*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SimpleDesign是一个直接在数据空间训练的多模态蛋白质设计模型，通过单阶段端到端目标（序列使用离散交叉熵、结构使用回归损失）结合Mixture-of-Transformer架构，在2M序列-结构对上训练，实现了强性能的蛋白质共设计与无条件生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质设计模型通常采用多阶段训练流程：先训练自编码器将数据token化为潜在表示，再在潜在空间上训练生成模型。作者假设这种多阶段训练并非必要，希望通过直接在数据空间进行单阶段训练来简化流程并提升性能。

Method: SimpleDesign采用单阶段端到端训练目标，序列模态使用离散交叉熵损失，结构模态使用回归损失。架构上提出了Mixture-of-Transformer，允许模态特定的处理同时保持跨两种模态的全局自注意力，以有效建模序列与结构模态之间的差异。模型在超过2M序列-结构对上训练。

Result: SimpleDesign在蛋白质共设计以及无条件序列/结构生成基准测试中均取得了强性能，验证了不依赖多阶段潜在空间训练即可获得高性能共设计模型的假设。

Conclusion: 多阶段训练并非蛋白质共设计模型的必要条件，直接在数据空间进行单阶段端到端训练是一种有效且更简洁的替代方案，为蛋白质生成模型的设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Proteins are fundamental to biological processes, with their function determined by the complex interplay between the amino acid sequence and the three-dimensional structure. Developing generative models capable of understanding this intrinsically multi-modal relationship is crucial for fields like drug discovery and protein engineering. Existing models often rely on a multi-stage training process where autoencoders that tokenize data into latent representations are trained in a first stage. Secondly, a generative model is trained on the latent representation of the autoencoder(s), i.e., generative modeling in a latent space. We hypothesize that this multi-stage training is not necessary to obtain performant co-design models and thus present SimpleDesign, an effective multi-modal protein design model trained directly in the data space. SimpleDesign leverages a single-stage end-to-end objective that combines discrete cross-entropy for sequences and a regression objective for structures. In order to effectively model the difference in sequence and structure modalities, we develop a Mixture-of-Transformer architecture that allows modality-specific processing while keeping global self-attention over both modalities. We train SimpleDesign on over 2M sequence-structure pairs achieving strong performance across co-design and unconditional sequence/structure generation benchmarks.

</details>


### [213] [RecurTrace: Adaptive Latent Reasoning with Loop-Time Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03379)
*Yuxiang Wang,Kunyu Feng,Yingda Shen,Haoning Xu,Junyu Wang,Zhizheng Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RecurTrace框架，通过循环内存注意力（Loop Memory Attention）让循环层沿循环时间轴回访早期计算，并利用停头（halting head）配合预言机监督实现可变循环次数，解决了推理深度固定和计算历史不可访问两个限制，在MathQA及多规模生成任务上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复中间层小模块可以在不增加参数或生成额外token的情况下提升语言模型的有效推理深度（潜在递归），但存在两个限制：每次迭代只能看到上一次输出而无法直接访问更早的计算；固定的循环次数对简单输入浪费深度，对困难输入计算不足。

Method: RecurTrace包含两个核心组件：1) Loop Memory Attention，让每个循环层沿循环时间轴关注自身之前迭代的状态，从而回访早期计算而非仅依赖最新状态；2) 停头（halting head）读取循环状态并预测是否继续循环，使用一个预言机提供监督信号，该预言机判断额外深度是否仍能降低损失。

Result: 在MathQA对照实验中，同一循环骨干网络上RecurTrace达到56.9%准确率（平均2.0次循环），超过固定循环深度最佳结果2.2个百分点（在匹配计算量下）。ACT和PonderNet退化为单次循环，CALM仅54.1%（5.6次循环），LoopUS-Conf和TaH-Mismatch分别为55.3%（3.2次循环）和55.7%（2.1次循环）。此外，在0.6B、1.7B、4B和8B规模上，RecurTrace相对同预算微调基线提升生成准确率，增益随模型规模从0.6增长到3.4个百分点。

Conclusion: RecurTrace通过利用循环轨迹本身的信息，同时解决了潜在递归中历史计算不可访问和计算量分配不均两个关键瓶颈，在数学推理和生成任务上均显著优于固定循环深度及现有的自适应循环方法（如ACT、PonderNet、CALM等），且增益随模型规模扩大而增大。

Abstract: Repeating a small block of middle layers increases a language model's effective inference depth without adding parameters or generating extra tokens, and recent work shows that this latent recurrence improves reasoning. However, two design choices limit these gains. Each iteration sees only the previous output and cannot directly access earlier computations. Moreover, a fixed loop count wastes depth on easy inputs while leaving hard ones with too little computation. We introduce RecurTrace, which addresses both limitations using the loop's own trajectory. Specifically, Loop Memory Attention lets each looped layer attend to its own states from previous iterations along the loop-time axis, so the model can revisit earlier computations instead of relying on the latest state alone. A halting head then reads the loop state and predicts whether to continue, with supervision from an oracle that identifies when additional depth still reduces loss. In a controlled MathQA comparison on the same looped backbone, RecurTrace achieves 56.9% accuracy with an average of 2.0 loops, exceeding the best fixed loop depth by 2.2 points at matched compute. By comparison, ACT and PonderNet collapse to one loop, and CALM reaches only 54.1% with 5.6 loops, while the stronger LoopUS-Conf and TaH-Mismatch baselines reach 55.3% at 3.2 loops and 55.7% at 2.1 loops. Finally, RecurTrace improves generation accuracy over same-budget fine-tuned baselines at 0.6B, 1.7B, 4B, and 8B, with the gain growing with model size from 0.6 to 3.4 points.

</details>


### [214] [TIGPO: Temporal Instance-Graph Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03383)
*Jinwei Gan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TIGPO通过跨策略更新的持久化转移图扩展了基于图的信用分配，在ALFWorld和WebShop上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的策略优化方法在每个策略更新周期内独立构建图，丢弃了早期策略发现的转移，导致优势估计局限于小规模的batch-local rollout组，无法充分利用历史经验进行信用分配。

Method: TIGPO维护每个任务的持久化转移图，使不同策略版本发现的合法转移能共同决定当前rollout的信用。该方法将固定rollout预算分配为Exploration slots（普通任务采样）和Revisit slots（回访已探索任务）。每次回访时，将当前rollout组与对应的早期Exploration组配对构建跨时间参考，以稳定小rollout组下的相对优势估计。历史转移和分数仅作为结构和统计参考，不参与策略损失的重放。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop基准上的实验表明，TIGPO一致优于先前的基于组和基于图的策略优化方法。

Conclusion: 跨策略更新维持持久化图并结合跨时间rollout参考，能够有效增强长视界LLM代理的信用分配，提升策略优化性能。

Abstract: Graph-based policy optimization improves credit assignment for long-horizon LLM agents by organizing rollout trajectories into state-transition graphs. However, existing methods construct graphs independently within each policy update, discarding transitions discovered by earlier policies and limiting advantage estimation to small, batch-local rollout groups. We propose \emph{Temporal Instance-Graph Policy Optimization} (TIGPO), which extends graph-based credit assignment across policy updates. TIGPO maintains a persistent transition graph for each task, allowing valid transitions discovered by different policy versions to jointly determine credit for current rollouts. To actively reconnect current exploration with historical experience, TIGPO allocates a fixed rollout budget between Exploration slots for ordinary task sampling and Revisit slots for delayed reattempts of previously explored tasks. For each revisit, TIGPO pairs the current rollout group with its corresponding earlier Exploration group to construct a cross-temporal reference. The enlarged reference is designed to stabilize relative advantage estimation under small rollout groups, while comparison on the same task directly captures policy improvement across training stages. Historical transitions and scores serve only as structural and detached statistical references and are never replayed in the policy loss. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop demonstrate that TIGPO consistently outperforms prior group-based and graph-based policy optimization methods.

</details>


### [215] [Inferred Generative-Process Diversity Predicts Correlated Failure Across Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03422)
*Ross Tieman,Evan Markou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用生成过程多样性（generative-process diversity）而非语义相似性来衡量多语言模型系统中模型间的多样性。通过将原始模型输出的归一化压缩距离（NCD）对置换对照做残差化，在38个语言模型上识别出语义相似性遗漏的群体结构，并能预测跨任务的关联性失败（correlated failure），优于语义相似性和能力差异的预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多样性是集体系统韧性的关键因素，但多样性的类型取决于系统特性和失效模式。现有研究用语义相似性评估语言模型群体的多样性，仅捕捉输出含义的差异，无法反映模型间行为和失败的强相关性。需要一个更根本的多样性概念——生成过程多样性，即生成观测输出的过程之间的差异。

Method: 借助算法信息论，使用模型原始输出的归一化压缩距离（NCD），并对置换对照做残差处理，以此作为推断生成过程多样性的度量。在38个语言模型和10个不重叠基准族上计算该度量，并与语义相似性和模型对能力进行比较分析。

Result: 该方法识别出语义相似性遗漏的群体结构。跨基准的部分秩关联为-0.216（95%区间[-0.309,-0.122]），在所有十个基准上估计均为负值，表明生成过程多样性的增加与模型对关联性失败的减少相关，且该效应无法归因于语义相似性或能力。

Conclusion: 推断生成过程多样性为研究多模型系统的多样性提供了新颖且实用的方法，特别适用于安全相关的应用场景。该方法超越了语义相似性，能更好地解释和预测模型对的关联性失败。

Abstract: Diversity is a widely observed factor in the resilient function of collective systems, yet the type of diversity that matters depends on the properties and failure modes of the system. This distinction is important for systems composed of multiple language models. Different models may be treated as independent components even when their behaviour and failures remain strongly correlated. Assessments of language-model populations using semantic similarity demonstrate limited semantic diversity, but this captures only differences in the meaning of observed outputs. We argue that a more fundamental notion of model diversity is generative-process diversity, the differences between processes capable of generating the observed outputs. Drawing from Algorithmic Information Theory, we use Normalised Compression Distance between raw model outputs, residualised against a permutation control, as a measure of inferred generative-process diversity. Across 38 language models, this measure identifies population structure missed by semantic similarity and predicts cross-task variation in chance-corrected correlated failure among model pairs across ten disjoint benchmark families, beyond semantic similarity and model-pair capability. The cross-benchmark partial rank association is $-0.216$ with a 95% interval of $[-0.309,-0.122]$, and the estimate is negative on all ten benchmarks. These results indicate that increased generative-process diversity is associated with reduced correlated failure in model pairs that is not attributable to semantic similarity or capability. Inferred generative-process diversity offers a novel and practical approach for investigating diversity of multi-model systems in safety-relevant contexts.

</details>


### [216] [TraveL: Transformer-based Multi-view Path Distributional Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03427)
*Fang He,Tao-yang Fu,Wang-chien Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Transformer的多视角分布表示学习框架（TraveL），用于道路网络的路径分布表示学习，通过编码多样化旅行者行为和区域相关性，在合成和真实数据集上优于现有的最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路径表示学习（PRL）工作通常利用路段与路径之间的共现关系学习向量表示，但未探索路径上多样化的旅行者行为以及路段之间的区域相关性，导致表示信息不够丰富。

Method: 提出TraveL框架，核心包括：(1) 将路径与旅行起始时间编码为分布表示，可解码出路径上的旅行者行为样本；(2) 通过分析区域相关性提出区域注意力机制，编码路段间的区域关联；(3) 利用Kolmogorov-Smirnov（K-S）检验比较采样的旅行者行为与真实数据以辅助训练。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上优于最先进方法：旅行时间分布估计的Mean K-S距离提升14.7%，路径相似性预测的MAE提升16.7%，目的地预测的MAE提升3.97%。

Conclusion: 通过分布表示而非点向量表示，结合区域相关性和K-S检验训练策略，能显著提升路径相关应用（如旅行时间估计、路径相似性预测、目的地预测）的性能，验证了分布表示在PRL中的有效性。

Abstract: Path representation learning (PRL) for road networks has received increasing research attention, due to various path-related applications. Existing works on PRL typically exploit the co-occurrence relationship among road segments and paths to learn a vector as the path representation, without exploring the varied traveler behaviors and the regional correlation on the path. In this work, we propose to learn distributional representations, which provide valuable information for use in path-related applications, by capturing the varied traveler behaviors as well as the various dependencies within regions of road segments. We propose a novel Transformer-based Multi-view Distributional Representation Learning (TraveL) framework to encode a path along with a travel starting time to a distributional representation, which can be used to decode possible samples of on-path traveler behavior. Moreover, by analyzing the regional correlation which reveals various road segment relationships, we propose a regional attention to encode these correlations in a path. Also, we explore the idea of Kolmogorov-Smirnov (K-S) test to compare the sampled traveler behavior against the collected ground truth to facilitate training. Experimental results show that the proposed TraveL model outperforms the state-of-the-art methods on both synthetic and real-world datasets, by 14.7% in Mean K-S distance for travel time distribution estimation, 16.7% in Mean Absolute Error (MAE) for path similarity prediction, and 3.97% in MAE for destination prediction.

</details>


### [217] [It's the Problem, Not the Path: Budget and Difficulty Confounds in LLM Reasoning Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03436)
*Yigit Utku Bulut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对大型语言模型推理轨迹中的"突破时刻"和"早期可见命运"两类解读进行了反事实控制检验。通过重启控制的截断探针和预注册、难度控制的测试，发现：在178个问题-模型单元中仅1个单元真正受到前缀限制；早期窗口内部信号不包含超出题目难度基线的可检测结果信息；公开语料上的高池化AUROC不能单独证明尝试内信息的存在，必须依赖问题基线或问题内评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于LLM推理轨迹中"突破时刻"（breakthrough moments）与"早期注定命运"（early-legible fates）的解读，都缺少在声明层面上的反事实对照。作者希望检验：(1) 当前缀被新计算替代时，所谓的"前缀价值"是否仍然存在；(2) 早期窗口的内部信号是否真的包含关于最终结果的信息，而非仅是问题难度的反映。

Method: 作者设计了两套对照：(1) 重启控制的截断探针（restart-controlled truncation probe）：比较按锚点继续生成的解决率与从头重启在匹配总生成token预算下的曲线，区分"前缀承载价值"与"恰好符合续写预算"；(2) 预注册的、难度控制的早期窗口信号测试，并辅以两个无需生成的分析公开语料的方法：一个轨迹盲（trace-blind）的难度代理指标，以及对最接近已发表正面结果的早期窗口探针进行紧密复现重构。

Result: 在178个问题-模型单元（89个MATH问题×两个小型开放模型）中，仅1/178个单元真正受前缀限制；重启剂量-反应可区分计算受限模型与能力受限模型；当匹配预算位于重启网格内时，9/9的情况下延续自身前缀优于重启，但主要是计算压缩而非可达性扩展。预注册测试未发现早期窗口内部信号包含超出题目难度基线的结果信息。轨迹盲的难度代理在192K DeepSeek-R1生成上达到AUROC 0.873（处于已发表探针范围内）；紧密复现的最接近已发表正面结果获得池化0.849，但问题内统计上在全部10个锚点均与随机水平无显著差异（t=4时为0.496）。事后针对目标探针仅发现小幅平均残余，集中于三个低失败率问题。

Conclusion: 高池化探针AUROC本身不能证明尝试内有信息；必须加入仅含问题（question-only）的基线或进行问题内评估。论文的核心主张是：许多被解读为推理"突破"或"早期注定"的现象，在反事实控制下可能仅是计算压缩或题目难度伪影，而非真正的前缀稀缺价值或早期可读的结果信号。

Abstract: Reasoning traces of large language models are widely read as containing "breakthrough" moments and early-legible fates. Both readings rest on measurements missing a counterfactual control at the level of the claim; we supply both controls. First, a restart-controlled truncation probe separates when a solution fits the continuation budget from when a prefix carries value that fresh computation cannot buy, comparing per-anchor continuation solve rates against from-scratch restart curves at matched total generated-token budget. Applied to 178 problem-model cells (89 MATH problems x two small open models, an outcome-blind but difficulty-targeted cohort), exactly 1 of 178 cells survives as prefix-limited; restart dose-response separates a compute-starved model from a capability-limited one; and wherever the matched budget lies inside the restart grid, continuing the model's own prefix beats restarting (9 of 9) -- predominantly compute compression rather than expanded reachability. Second, a pre-registered, difficulty-controlled test finds no detectable outcome information in early-window internal signals beyond a problem-difficulty baseline, and two generation-free analyses of public corpora show why this control is needed: a trace-blind difficulty proxy reaches AUROC 0.873 on 192K DeepSeek-R1 generations -- inside the published probe range -- and a closely matched reconstruction of the closest published early-window positive recovers a comparable pooled result (0.849) while within problem it is statistically indistinguishable from chance at all ten anchors (0.496 at t=4); a post-hoc within-targeted probe finds only a small average residual, concentrated in three low-failure problems. High pooled probe AUROCs cannot by themselves establish within-attempt information; a question-only baseline or within-problem evaluation is required.

</details>


### [218] [Guide, Not Bind: Why Defeasible Priors Fail in Augmented Lagrangian Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03442)
*Sairam Sundararaman,Sara Girdhar,Manit Narasimha Murthy,Samrudh N,Bhaskarjyoti Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出可微因果发现中"指导而非绑定"的先验设计存在两类独立缺陷：其一，顺序惩罚递增的ALM机制会在反事实检查前抑制被错误禁止的真实边（DADU违反了文中给出的三个必要条件）；其二，即使修复机制，标准相关匹配目标在理论上使真实边与反向边成本完全相等（2r²），而协方差匹配能以至少w₀⁴的余量区分两个方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微因果发现方法普遍将专家先验编码为禁边约束，并通过增广拉格朗日（ALM）惩罚强制执行，假设数据自适应的松弛机制能够折扣并最终覆盖数据持续反驳的规则。作者要验证这一"指导而非绑定"的设计是否真的有效，并揭示其潜在的根本性失败模式。

Method: 作者通过理论分析与大规模实验相结合的方式：1）提出并分析了DADU这一自适应松弛规则，给出任何自适应松弛必须满足的三个必要条件（命题），并证明DADU违反全部三条（推论）；2）对标准相关匹配目标进行封闭形式推导，证明真实边与反向边的成本相等（引理：相等关系），并证明协方差匹配具备分离方向的能力（引理：分离性）。实验涵盖4到32节点图，共3,072次训练运行。

Result: 实验结果证实：在DADU机制下，单个错误先验在87-97%的试验中抑制了真实边。理论研究揭示了两个独立的原因：机制层面，顺序惩罚递增的ALM在反事实检查能触发之前就压制了被错误禁止的真实边；目标函数层面，归一化为相关性丢弃了方差信息（该信息本可使等方差模型变得可识别），导致相关匹配目标无法区分边的方向，而协方差匹配可以做到。

Conclusion: "指导而非绑定"的设计从根本上存在缺陷，且这种缺陷来自两个独立层面：ALM惩罚机制的自适应松弛在时序上无法及时纠正错误先验，而相关匹配目标在空间上消除了边的方向可识别性。修复这两个问题只能部分恢复其有效性，提示需要根本性的设计变更。

Abstract: Differentiable causal discovery methods increasingly encode expert priors as forbidden-edge constraints enforced by an Augmented Lagrangian (ALM) penalty, on the assumption that a data-adaptive relaxation mechanism will discount and eventually override a rule the data consistently contradicts. We show this design, which we call \emph{guide, not bind}, fails for two independent, precisely characterized reasons, and that directly repairing both restores it only partially. First, sequential penalty-ramping ALM suppresses a wrongly-forbidden true edge before any counterfactual check can detect it: we give three necessary conditions any adaptive relaxation must satisfy to avoid this (Proposition~\ref{prop:conditions}), prove that DADU---the natural relaxation rule this paper introduces as the object of study---violates all three (Corollary~\ref{cor:dadu_failure}), and confirm the failure across 3{,}072 training runs spanning graphs from 4 to 32 nodes, where a single wrong prior suppresses a true edge in 87--97\% of trials under DADU. Second, and independent of any fix to the mechanism, we prove in closed form that the standard correlation-matching objective ties a true edge and its reverse to an identical cost of exactly $2r^2$ (Lemma~\ref{lem:tie}), not because the underlying equal-variance model is unidentifiable, but because normalizing to correlation discards exactly the variance information that would make it identifiable; covariance matching instead separates the two directions by a provable margin of at least $w_0^4$ (Lemma~\ref{lem:separation}).

</details>


### [219] [Beyond Straightness: Non-Crossing Flow Matching via Quantile AlignTree Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03443)
*Junyi Lin,Mengyu Li,Jingxuan Hu,Kejun He,Cheng Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QAT-FM，一种基于分位数对齐树结构的高效结构化耦合策略，用于Flow Matching生成模型，以较低成本构建高斯先验与目标分布间的分层耦合，缓解独立耦合导致的路径交叉与局部速度模糊问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow Matching的性能高度依赖源分布与目标分布之间耦合的质量，但独立耦合会导致路径交叉和局部速度模糊，而基于OT的耦合又通常构建成本过高，需要一种高效且高质量的结构化耦合方法。

Method: QAT-FM通过分位数对齐的树结构在高斯先验与目标数据分布之间构建分层耦合，耦合构建时间复杂度为O(Nd log N)，支持每个配对源的采样复杂度为O(d)。理论上证明了QAT耦合满足边缘一致性、诱导无交叉的线性插值路径，并持续改善中间时刻的路径分离度。该方法还可自然扩展到条件生成场景。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，QAT-FM在保持竞争力的生成性能的同时，大幅降低了耦合构建成本。

Conclusion: QAT-FM提供了一种在耦合质量与构建成本之间取得良好平衡的结构化耦合方案，既缓解了独立耦合的路径问题，又避免了OT耦合的高昂代价，并支持大规模高维生成任务的扩展。

Abstract: The performance of Flow Matching largely depends on the quality of the coupling between the source and target distributions. However, independent coupling often leads to path crossings and local velocity ambiguity, while OT-based couplings typically incur high construction costs. To address this challenge, we propose Quantile AlignTree Flow Matching (QAT-FM), an efficient structured coupling strategy that constructs a hierarchical coupling between a Gaussian prior and the target data distribution via a quantile-aligned tree structure. QAT-FM constructs the coupling in $\mathcal{O}(Nd\log N)$ time and supports per-pair source sampling with $\mathcal{O}(d)$ complexity, enabling scalable training for large-scale high-dimensional generative tasks. Theoretically, we prove that the QAT coupling satisfies marginal consistency, induces non-crossing linear interpolation paths, and consistently improves path separation at intermediate times compared with independent coupling, thereby alleviating local velocity ambiguity. QAT-FM further extends naturally to conditional generation, enabling structured conditional coupling while preserving global Gaussian alignment. Experiments across diverse benchmark datasets demonstrate that QAT-FM achieves competitive generative performance while substantially reducing coupling construction cost.

</details>


### [220] [A Two-Stage Forecasting System for CPU Workload Prediction in Private Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03457)
*Ashir Javeed,Anton Borg,Håkan Grahn,Lars Lundberg,Dhyey Patel,Sogand Shirinbab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段集成预测模型，先预测客户服务请求量（TPS），再基于TPS预测估计未来CPU工作负载，采用级联XGBoost架构并辅以自适应在线重训练（扩展窗口策略）应对概念漂移。在私有云环境中10个应用的真实轨迹上评估，多数应用SMAPE低于7%，最佳应用MAE为0.7372、RMSE为1.1866、SMAPE为3.57%、R²为0.9185，60步预测范围内误差累积可控，优于传统直接CPU预测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云资源预测方法大多直接从历史资源迹线预测CPU负载，忽略了客户服务需求与后续资源消耗之间的内在关联，导致预测准确性和鲁棒性受限。本研究旨在通过显式建模"服务请求→资源消耗"的依赖关系来提升预测性能。

Method: 提出两阶段级联集成预测框架：第一阶段使用XGBoost预测未来客户服务请求（TPS）；第二阶段将TPS预测结果作为输入，再用XGBoost估计未来CPU工作负载。两个组件采用级联学习架构，并通过扩展窗口策略进行自适应在线重训练以解决云工作负载中的概念漂移问题。

Result: 在包含10个应用的私有云真实轨迹数据集上，多数应用的SMAPE低于7%。最佳应用达到MAE=0.7372、RMSE=1.1866、SMAPE=3.57%、R²=0.9185。水平方向漂移分析显示在60步预测范围内具有稳定的递归预测行为和受控的误差累积。与直接CPU预测方法相比，该模型在预测鲁棒性、计算效率和可解释性方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 两阶段集成预测模型通过显式建模服务需求与资源消耗的因果关系，为云环境中的主动资源管理和智能自动扩缩容提供了一种更鲁棒、高效且可解释的预测方案。

Abstract: Accurate cloud resource forecasting is essential for proactive resource provisioning, maintaining Quality of Service (QoS), and reducing operational costs in dynamic cloud environments. The existing forecasting approaches predominantly estimate future CPU workload directly from historical resource traces, which often overlook the relationship between customer service demand and subsequent resource consumption. This study proposes a two-stage integrated forecasting model that explicitly models this dependency by first forecasting customer service requests, expressed as Transactions Per Second (TPS), and subsequently estimating future CPU workload from the TPS forecast. Both the forecasting component and resource prediction component employed the XGBoost model within a cascaded learning architecture, complemented by adaptive online retraining using an expanding-window strategy to address concept drift in continuously evolving cloud workloads. The proposed work was evaluated using real-world traces collected from a private cloud environment comprising ten applications. Experimental results demonstrate robust forecasting performance by achieving Symmetric Mean Absolute Percentage Error (SMAPE) below $7\%$ for most applications, with the best-performing application achieving an MAE of $0.7372$, RMSE of $1.1866$, SMAPE of $3.57\%$, and an R2 of $0.9185$. Horizon-wise drift analysis confirmed stable recursive forecasting behavior with controlled error accumulation across a 60-step prediction horizon. Compared with the conventional direct CPU forecasting method, the proposed two-stage integrated model gives improved forecasting robustness, computational efficiency, and interpretability, making it well-suited for proactive resource management and intelligent auto-scaling in cloud computing environments.

</details>


### [221] [Mind the Gap: Robustness Risks in PII Detection Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03464)
*Adeel Zafar,Slawomir Nowaczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现现代PII检测系统在标准基准上表现良好，但在部署中面临的真实分布偏移下存在实质性鲁棒性失效。作者构建了一个覆盖七类自然分布偏移的压力测试基准，评估三类主流架构（SpaCy、Presidio、Qwen2.5-3B），发现它们各自有不同且互补的失效模式，并提出了一种带QA反馈循环的混合检测管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PII检测是数据保护基础设施的基础组件，漏检直接构成隐私和安全风险。尽管现代PII系统在标准基准上报告强大性能，这些评估掩盖了实际部署中在分布偏移条件下的实质性鲁棒性缺陷。作者旨在揭示不同PII检测范式在噪声、非结构化和非正式输入下的失效本质，而非简单地比较最先进精度。

Method: 构建跨七个自然分布偏移类别的压力测试基准，评估三个代表性系统：基于编码器的NER（SpaCy）、基于规则的混合检测（Presidio）和生成式LLM提取（Qwen2.5-3B）。基于发现的差异化失效模式，提出带QA驱动的反馈循环的混合检测管线以实现迭代风险缓解。

Result: 三种架构在分布外输入上均表现显著退化，但失效模式不同且互补：编码器模型主要在处理未见过的表面形式和边界检测上失败；规则系统在非标准输入格式上失效；LLM则出现实体类型混淆和生成不稳定问题。这表明总体基准分数掩盖了部署关键性的弱点，且没有任何单一架构在所有PII类别上均匀可靠。

Conclusion: 聚合基准分数不足以反映PII系统的部署可靠性，不同架构的失效模式各有差异且互补。为应对这一问题，作者提出了混合检测管线并结合QA驱动的反馈循环，并公开发布压力测试基准以支持面向分布偏移的PII系统评估。

Abstract: Personally Identifiable Information (PII) detection is a foundational component of data protection infrastructure where missed entities constitute direct privacy and security risks. Although modern PII systems report strong performance on standard benchmarks, we show that these evaluations mask substantial robustness failures under realistic distribution shifts encountered in deployment. Rather than comparing state-of-the-art accuracy, we study how different PII detection paradigms fail under noisy, unstructured, and informal inputs. We construct a stress test benchmark spanning seven categories of natural distribution shift and evaluate representative systems from three widely deployed architectural families: encoder-based NER (SpaCy), rule-based hybrid detection (Presidio), and generative LLM extraction (Qwen2.5-3B).
  All three exhibit significant degradation on out-of-distribution inputs, but with distinct and complementary failure modes. Encoder models primarily fail on unseen surface forms and boundary detection, rule-based systems fail on non-standard formats, and LLMs exhibit entity-type confusion and generation instability. These results show that aggregate benchmark scores obscure deployment-critical weaknesses and that no single architecture is uniformly reliable across PII categories. Motivated by these findings, we propose a hybrid detection pipeline with a QA-driven feedback loop for iterative risk mitigation, and release our benchmark to support OOD-aware evaluation of PII systems.

</details>


### [222] [Spectral characteristics of autoencoder parameters as a vector representation of data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03495)
*Maria Nikitina,Anton Bishuk,Oleg Bakhteev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过理论分析与实验验证，证明自编码器模型的参数（特别是参数矩阵的奇异值谱特征）可以作为对应训练数据样本的稠密向量表示，能够在不使用原始数据的情况下高精度区分由不同数据子集训练的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索模型参数与训练数据统计特性之间的深层关联，提出一种不依赖原始样本或复杂向量生成算法、仅基于自编码器参数谱特征构建样本表示的方法，以验证"模型参数即样本表示"这一假设。

Method: 首先从理论上分析自编码器参数矩阵的奇异值与训练数据协方差矩阵特征值之间的关系；随后基于参数矩阵的谱特征构建向量表示，并在CIFAR-10与FashionMNIST数据集上进行实验，验证该向量表示对不同数据子集训练模型的区分能力。

Result: 理论分析表明自编码器参数矩阵的奇异值与训练数据协方差矩阵的特征值存在对应关系；实验证明基于参数谱特征构建的向量表示能以高精度区分在不同数据子集上训练的模型，且无需复杂算法或原始数据。

Conclusion: 训练后的自编码器参数可以被视为训练样本的有效表示，参数空间与数据空间之间存在可传递的信息通道，这为模型参数分析与数据表征提供了新的视角。

Abstract: This paper examines the relationship between the parameters of autoencoder models and the statistical properties of the data on which they are trained. Autoencoders are defined as models with an encoder-decoder architecture, trained to reconstruct input data through a compressed latent representation. It is proposed that the model parameters can be viewed as a dense vector representation of the corresponding sample. To test this hypothesis, a theoretical and experimental study is conducted in which a vector representation is formed based on the spectral characteristics of the autoencoder parameter matrices. Theoretical analysis shows that the singular values of the model parameter matrices are related to the eigenvalues of the covariance matrix of the training data, ensuring the transfer of information between the data space and the parameter space. Experimental results on the CIFAR-10 and FashionMNIST datasets confirm that the resulting vector representations allow for a high degree of accuracy in distinguishing between models trained on different data subsets, without resorting to complex vector generation algorithms or using the original samples. These results suggest that the parameters of trained autoencoders can be viewed as sample representations.

</details>


### [223] [Restricted Eigenvalues Beyond Gaussian Width: Threshold Occupancy under Heavy Tails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03504)
*Shi Fu,Huibo Xu,Qixin Zhang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对COLT 2015开放问题（Banerjee et al., 2015）给出了系统性否定回答：在仅满足均匀小球条件（uniform small-ball condition）的重尾测量设计中，弥散锥上的限制特征值（RE）界并不遵循各向同性次高斯情形下由高斯宽度决定的样本复杂度定律。作者识别出关键障碍为"同时阈值占用"（simultaneous threshold occupancy），并给出精细的极坏案例样本复杂度刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 限制特征值（RE）界是范数正则化估计器稳定恢复的关键条件。已有理论表明，对各向同性次高斯测量，基准样本量为1+w(A)^2（w(A)为归一化弥散锥的高斯宽度）。Banerjee等人在COLT 2015开放问题中提出：仅由均匀小球条件出发，重尾测量是否也能获得相同的样本复杂度定律？本文旨在回答这一悬而未决的问题。

Method: 作者通过构造反例论证问题的否定性答案。核心构造包括：(1) 构造具有常宽度多面体弥散锥，在固定小球常数下，直到环境维度的一半，每条样本路径上的经验RE为零；(2) 证明任意有限值域空间可在任意窄球冠上实现精确阈值编码，并可提升为完整的多面体弥散锥截面；(3) 对固定阈值VC维d，刻画当β→0时极坏样本复杂度为Θ(β^{-1}[d log(1/β)+log(1/δ)])；(4) 通过高斯平滑构造处处正的C^∞密度但RE仍然任意差的重尾分布。

Result: (1) 原问题在"全维度自由、任意集合"形式下不成立，缺失的障碍是同时阈值占用；(2) 常宽度多面体弥散锥在n ≤ p/2（p为环境维度）时，每条样本路径的经验RE为零；(3) 对于固定阈值VC维d，作为β→0，极坏样本复杂度为Θ(β^{-1}[d log(1/β)+log(1/δ)])；(4) 分离现象即便在精确各向同性和所有有限矩条件下依然存在：高斯测量O(1+log(1/δ))样本成功，而各向同性重尾设计在n≲√(p/log p)时路径wise失败；(5) 在高斯平滑下可得到密度C^∞处处为正但仍具有任意差RE的分布；(6) 在各向同性条件下，由仿射维度乘平方包围半径给出的无分布（distribution-free）替代界在该族上是尖锐的。

Conclusion: 各向同性次高斯测量下基于高斯宽度的RE定律不能推广到仅由均匀小球条件限定的重尾设计。需要对重尾情形寻求不同的（分布自由的）界，例如由仿射维度与平方包围半径刻画的替代条件，这类条件在该问题上被证明是尖锐的。

Abstract: Restricted eigenvalue (RE) bounds govern stable recovery by norm-regularized estimators. For isotropic sub-Gaussian measurements, the benchmark sample size is $1+w(A)^2$, where $w(A)$ is the Gaussian width of the normalized descent cone. The COLT 2015 open-problem note (Banerjee et al., 2015) asked whether the same law follows for heavy-tailed designs from a uniform small-ball condition alone. We give an explicit and systematic negative answer to the general question as formulated there: the proposed law fails in its full dimension-free, arbitrary-set form, and the missing obstruction is simultaneous threshold occupancy. A constant-width polyhedral descent cone with fixed small-ball constants has zero empirical RE on every sample path up to half the ambient dimension. More generally, every finite range space admits exact threshold encoding in an arbitrarily narrow spherical cap and a lift to a full polyhedral descent-cone section. For every fixed threshold VC dimension $d$, as $β\downarrow0$, the sharp worst-case sample complexity is $Θ(β^{-1}[d\log(1/β)+\log(1/δ)])$. The separation persists under exact isotropy and all finite moments: on the same constant-width cone, Gaussian measurements succeed with $O(1+\log(1/δ))$ samples, whereas an isotropic heavy-tailed design fails pathwise for $n\lesssim\sqrt{p/\log p}$. Gaussian smoothing yields an everywhere-positive $C^\infty$ density while retaining arbitrarily poor RE. Under isotropy, a distribution-free fallback governed by affine dimension times squared enclosing radius is sharp on this family.

</details>


### [224] [An Adversarial Zero-Shot Learning Approach for Anomaly Detection in Multivariate IoT Traffic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03505)
*Mahshid Rezakhani,Tolunay Seyfi,Fatemeh Afghah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于序列VAE架构、结合对抗学习与对比损失的多变量时间序列异常检测框架，实现在无标签数据和原始特征迁移条件下对IoT网络的零样本域适应，并通过编码器/解码器适配层和目的地分段策略应对异构IoT部署，在六个数据集、44个迁移场景上验证了强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT网络异常检测面临设备多样性、缺乏标注数据以及跨环境域差异三大挑战。现有方法通常需要标签数据或原始特征迁移，难以在隐私受限的真实IoT场景下实现有效的跨域异常检测。

Method: 该方法基于序列VAE架构，联合优化对抗学习与对比损失：(1)通过对抗学习获得域不变的潜在表示；(2)通过对比损失构建语义结构化的嵌入空间；(3)引入编码器和解码器适配层以对齐跨域特征分布；(4)提出基于目的地的分段策略以更好建模IoT流量中的真实通信结构。整个过程无需标注数据和原始特征迁移。

Result: 在覆盖工业、企业、通用、智能家居和军事自动化等领域的六个数据集上，通过44个跨域迁移场景进行全面评估。结果表明该方法在多个跨域设置中展现出强零样本泛化能力，并在现实、异构、隐私受限的IoT条件下与对比域适应基线相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了IoT场景下无标签、跨域异常检测的难题，通过对抗学习与对比损失的联合优化及适配层设计，实现了良好的零样本域适应能力，为隐私受限的真实IoT环境提供了可行的异常检测方案。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in Internet of Things (IoT) networks presents unique challenges due to the diversity of devices, lack of labeled data, and domain variability across environments. In this paper, we propose a novel framework for multivariate time-series anomaly detection that leverages adversarial learning and contrastive loss within a sequence-based Variational Autoencoder (VAE) architecture. Our method enables zero-shot domain adaptation by jointly optimizing domain-invariant latent representations and semantically structured embedding spaces, without requiring labeled data or raw feature transfer. To address the heterogeneity of IoT deployments, we introduce encoder and decoder adaptor layers that align feature distributions across domains while preserving contextual semantics. Additionally, we propose a destination-based segmentation strategy to better model real-world communication structures in IoT traffic. Our framework is comprehensively evaluated on six distinct datasets spanning industrial, enterprise, general-purpose, smart home, and military automation domains across 44 transfer scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate strong zero-shot generalization in several cross-domain settings and competitive performance against a contrastive domain-adaptation baseline under realistic, heterogeneous, and privacy-constrained IoT conditions.

</details>


### [225] [LongCounsel-8: A Benchmark Suite for Longitudinal Depression Tracking from Multi-Session Counseling Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03507)
*Jiayi Li,Zhaomin Wu,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 LongCounsel-8，一个包含 7,749 条五次咨询轨迹的纵向抑郁咨询基准套件，用于从多会话心理咨询对话中追踪抑郁症状严重程度及其跨会话变化，并通过实验揭示了现有方法的三个关键局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从多会话咨询对话中追踪抑郁需要同时估计当前症状严重程度及其跨会话变化，但受限于缺乏带有标准化会话级抑郁标签的纵向咨询数据。现有资源要么提供无抑郁标签的多会话对话，要么提供单会话的有标签访谈，无法支持纵向抑郁追踪任务的基准评估。

Method: LongCounsel-8 包含三个独立生成的子数据集，共 7,749 条五次会话的咨询轨迹。构建方法结合了三条技术路径：(1) 基于真实客户档案的仿真（profile-grounded simulation）以维持纵向一致性与多样性；(2) 基于经验数据驱动的状态构建（empirically informed state construction）以将症状进展锚定在真实模式上；(3) 间接行为实现（indirect behavioral realization）以自然表达受控的抑郁状态而不暴露目标标签。

Result: 实验发现：(1) 更低的单会话评分误差并不能保证趋势（改善或恶化）识别的准确性；(2) 现有方法在恶化轨迹上的一致性可靠性较低；(3) 额外的会话历史可能反而降低趋势预测的准确性。此外，模拟的自报告能较好地恢复受控状态，支持标签保真度。

Conclusion: 这些发现确立了 LongCounsel-8 作为推动抑郁评估从静态单会话预测走向可靠纵向心理健康变化追踪的基础资源。

Abstract: Tracking depression from multi-session counseling dialogues requires estimating both current symptom severity and how it changes across sessions. Yet progress on this task is constrained by the scarcity of longitudinal counseling data with standardized session-level depression labels. Existing resources typically provide either multi-session conversations without depression labels or labeled interviews in a single session. Building such a benchmark poses three challenges: maintaining longitudinal consistency and diversity, grounding symptom progression in empirical patterns, and expressing controlled depression states naturally without exposing target labels. To address these challenges, we introduce LongCounsel-8, a benchmark suite of three independently generated datasets totaling 7,749 five-session counseling trajectories, grounded in real-world client profiles, depression trajectories, symptom compositions, and counseling patterns. We combine profile-grounded simulation, empirically informed state construction, and indirect behavioral realization to address these challenges. Across the benchmark, simulated self-reports closely recover the controlled states, supporting label fidelity. Experiments on existing depression tracking methods reveal three key findings: (1) lower single-session score error does not guarantee accurate identification of trend, i.e., improvement or worsening; (2) existing methods are consistently less reliable on worsening trajectories; and (3) additional session history may reduce the accuracy of trend prediction. Together, these findings establish LongCounsel-8 as a foundation for advancing depression assessment from static, single-session prediction toward reliable longitudinal tracking of mental-health change.

</details>


### [226] [LeanGRPO: Eliminating Redundant Recomputation in Diffusion RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03528)
*Sijie Wang,Zhiqiang Tan,Xinrui Yang,Shaohuai Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeanGRPO通过重构数据并行布局并引入两种免重算训练调度（Retain和Reweight），消除了扩散强化学习（如DanceGRPO/FlowGRPO）中rollout与更新阶段对选定时间步的冗余梯度重算，在FLUX.1-dev和Wan上实现高达1.83倍的端到端加速，同时保持原始优化目标不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散RL方法（如DanceGRPO和FlowGRPO）在rollout后会对选定时间步使用梯度跟踪重新计算，这在同后端on-policy训练下是数学上冗余的。虽然直觉上rollout和策略更新可复用同一前馈骨干以避免冗余计算，但这会带来rollout阶段巨大的内存开销，因此需要一种无需重算且内存可控的高效训练方案。

Method: 提出LeanGRPO，核心包括：（1）重构数据并行布局以降低内存开销；（2）引入两种免重算的训练调度：LeanGRPO-Retain在rollout阶段开启梯度跟踪并直接复用计算图与已保存激活用于更新反向传播，免去重算；LeanGRPO-Reweight同样在rollout阶段启用梯度，但对每个选定步用临时优势值即时反传并延迟梯度同步，待轨迹完成后用真实优势值修正临时梯度。两种调度分别针对不同模型规模和输入尺寸进行优化。

Result: 在FlowGRPO/DanceGRPO框架下，基于FLUX.1-dev和Wan模型进行实验，LeanGRPO实现了高达1.83倍的端到端加速，同时完全保持原始优化目标。

Conclusion: LeanGRPO证明了通过合理的调度设计，可以在扩散RL训练中消除rollout与更新间的计算冗余，在不改变优化目标的前提下显著提升端到端训练效率，为大规模图像/视频生成模型的post-training提供了更高效的方案。

Abstract: Diffusion reinforcement learning (RL) has recently achieved significant success in post-training image and video generative models. However, most diffusion RL methods, including DanceGRPO and FlowGRPO, recompute selected timesteps with gradient tracking after rollout. Under on-policy training with the same backend for rollout and update, this recomputation is mathematically redundant. Intuitively, the rollout and policy update steps can reuse the same feed-forward backbone to avoid redundant computation, but doing so can incur a large memory overhead during rollout. To address the issue, we present LeanGRPO by restructuring the data-parallel layout and introducing two recompute-free training schedules for trajectory-logprob diffusion RL: (1) LeanGRPO-Retain enables gradient tracking during rollout and directly reuses the resulting computation graphs and saved activations for backward during update, requiring no recomputation; and (2) LeanGRPO-Reweight also enables gradients during rollout, but immediately backpropagates each selected step using a provisional advantage and delays gradient synchronization, then corrects the provisional gradients with the true advantage after the trajectory is completed. These schedules target different model scales and input sizes. Across FlowGRPO/DanceGRPO with FLUX.1-dev and Wan, LeanGRPO achieves up to 1.83x end-to-end speedup while preserving the original optimization objective.

</details>


### [227] [Coupled Scaling: A Representational Accessibility Framework for Neural Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03533)
*Jie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"耦合缩放"(Coupled Scaling)框架，认为神经网络的有限预算缩放不仅取决于数据几何或谱结构，还取决于架构-优化系统所能高效到达的表征几何与任务结构之间的关系。在可解的模式截断模型中，推导出残差指数位于由累积尾部与覆盖对数速率决定的区间内，并给出了具体指数公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经缩放理论要么从数据几何推导，要么假设给定的数据-模型谱，但无法解释同一数据在不同架构或优化设置下训练时缩放行为不同的现象。需要一个能统一描述任务结构、架构支持空间与有限预算获取之间相互作用的框架。

Method: 引入任务条件下的耦合缩放框架，构建可解的模式截断(solvable mode-truncation)模型。在该模型中，损失分解为架构支持空间之外的目标能量与未解析的支持尾部两部分。通过分析任意优先级顺序下的残差行为，定义累计尾部对数速率γ和覆盖对数速率ρ，推导残差指数所在区间。在固定核的简化情形下，从任务加权谱测度的近零尾部直接推导训练时间指数。

Result: 残差指数位于[ργ, γ]区间内；在有界离前缀增益条件下，完成前缀为主要限制因素，指数α=ργ；当谱系数按幂律衰减时，α=ρ(b-1)。框架区分了架构支持与有限预算获取两个层面，并提出两个可测试预测：静态任务相关几何应在共同预算下追踪损失，多尺度几何应追踪耦合特异性的指数排序（包括不同任务间的反转现象）。

Conclusion: 耦合缩放框架将神经缩放理解为任务结构、架构支持的几何可达性与优化获取过程三者耦合的结果。通过审计已发布的涌现轨迹，识别出进行直接析因测试所需的控制条件，以将几何测量与缩放拟合分离，为检验该理论提供了具体实验方案。

Abstract: Existing theories derive neural scaling from data geometry or a specified data-model spectrum, but systems trained on the same data can scale differently when architecture or optimization changes the representations they can efficiently reach. We introduce Coupled Scaling, a task-conditioned framework in which finite-budget scaling depends on the relation between task structure and the geometry accessible to an architecture-optimization system. In a solvable mode-truncation model, loss separates into target energy outside architectural support and an unresolved supported tail. For an arbitrary priority order, the residual lies between the best-N supported tail and the tail beyond the largest completed high-value prefix. If the cumulative-tail and coverage log-rates are $γ_{A,T}$ and $ρ_{A,O,T}$, the residual exponent lies in $[ρ_{A,O,T}γ_{A,T},γ_{A,T}]$. Under bounded off-prefix gain, the completed prefix is rate-determining and $α_{A,O,T}=ρ_{A,O,T}γ_{A,T}$; for $a_{A,T,j}\asymp j^{-b_{A,T}}$, this gives $α_{A,O,T}=ρ_{A,O,T}(b_{A,T}-1)$. A fixed-kernel specialization derives the training-time exponent from the near-zero tail of a task-weighted spectral measure defined independently of the loss fit. The framework separates architectural support from finite-budget acquisition and motivates two tests: static task-relevant geometry should track loss at a common budget, while multiscale geometry should track coupling-specific exponent ordering, including reversal across contrasting tasks. An audit of released emergence trajectories identifies the controls needed for a direct factorial test that measures geometry separately from the scaling fit.

</details>


### [228] [WeatherNext 3: Increasing resolution and performance of global weather models with raw observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03582)
*Stephan Rasp,Boris Babenko,Dominic Masters,Andrew El-Kadi,Samier Merchant,Guy Shalev,Ilan Price,Fred Zyda,Remi Lam,Sasha Shysheya,Matthew Willson,Stratis Markou,Shreya Agrawal,Suhani Vora,Mohammed Alewi Hassen,Sunny Mak,Tom R. Andersson,Megan Bela,Akib Uddin,Nofar Peled Levi,Ben Gaiarin,Ferran Alet,Aaron Bell,Peter Battaglia,Alvaro Sanchez-Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WeatherNext 3是一个新一代概率性中期天气预报AI模型，通过每小时更新、0.1度空间分辨率、0.25度用于多层级变量，直接吸收低延迟地球同步卫星数据、卫星衍生的降水估计、热带气旋和站点观测，解决了现有AI天气模型时空分辨率低和仅依赖分析数据的问题，建立了中期预报技能的新SOTA，并能对任意位置和时间的2米温度和露点进行高精度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的AI天气模型虽然在中期预报技能和计算效率上表现出色，但存在两个关键缺陷：一是其预报的时空分辨率低于最佳物理模型，二是它们仅用分析数据初始化和训练，无法直接利用观测数据，任何分析偏差都会被预报继承。此外较差的传统AI模型也无法生成太阳辐射、云量等每小时变量，且对站点观测的预测能力有限。

Method: WeatherNext 3通过三个主要创新解决上述问题：(1) 摄入低延迟地球同步卫星数据，实现每小时（而非6小时）生成一次新预报；(2) 提升时空分辨率至与物理全球模型相当——单层级变量（如太阳辐射和云量）达0.1度分辨率和小时级时间步长；(3) 超越传统分析变量，学习预测卫星衍生的降水估计、热带气旋和站点观测，利用稀疏站点数据的建模实现对任意地点和时间的2米温度/露点预测，并条件于局部地理特征。

Result: WeatherNext 3在概率性中期预报技能上建立了新SOTA。其2米温度和露点预报在包括未见站点的评估中误差显著低于竞争全球模型；每小时更新能力和高时空分辨率使其在预报粒度和性能上超越现有方法。

Conclusion: WeatherNext 3将运营级AI天气预报从单纯模拟数据同化、预报和后处理的传统不同阶段中解放出来，实现了端到端的一体化预报能力，进一步推动了全球天气预报在性能和粒度方面的前沿。

Abstract: State-of-the-art AI weather models have shown impressive medium-range forecast skill and computational efficiency, but suffer two key shortcomings: their forecasts have lower spatial and temporal resolution than the best physics-based models and they are exclusively initialized with and trained on analysis data. As a result, they cannot directly make use of observations, and any biases in the analysis are inherited by the forecast. WeatherNext 3 addresses these shortcomings and establishes a new state-of-the-art for probabilistic medium-range forecasting skill. First, WeatherNext 3 generates new forecasts every hour (rather than every 6 hours like traditional global models) by ingesting low-latency geostationary satellite data. Second, WeatherNext 3's temporal and spatial resolution are on par with physics-based global models, with hourly time steps and 0.1 degree resolution for single-level variables, including solar radiation and cloud cover. Third, WeatherNext 3 moves beyond traditional analysis variables by learning to predict satellite-derived precipitation estimates, as well as tropical cyclone and station observations. Modelling sparse station data allows WeatherNext 3 to make 2m temperature and dewpoint predictions at any location and time, conditioned on local geographical features, with substantially lower error than competing global models, even when evaluated against unseen stations. Together, WeatherNext 3's capabilities move operational AI-based weather forecasting beyond emulating the traditionally distinct stages of data assimilation, forecasting and post-processing, which helps to further push the frontier of performance and granularity for global weather prediction.

</details>


### [229] [Neural-Network Maxent: a general extension with learned nonlinearity, applied to time-series for Desert Locust distribution modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03603)
*Alessandro Grassi,Edoardo Kimani Bellotto,Wassim El Azami,Sabrina Outmani,Maximilien Houel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RNN Maxent，将Maxent框架中的固定特征字典替换为GRU神经网络，用于沙漠蝗虫栖息地适宜性建模。该方法保留Maxent的存在点统计基础与背景归一化，仅通过学习数据中的非线性关系替代预设特征变换。实验表明，基于50天环境时序数据（ERA5 Land、MODIS、Sentinel 3），RNN Maxent在ROC AUC（0.862 vs 0.792）和F1（0.671 vs 0.590）上均优于标准Maxent。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Maxent依赖手工选择的特征变换的线性组合，无法捕捉生态监测中常见的非线性时序关系。当协变量（降水、土壤湿度、植被指数）随时间演变时，标准实现将时间序列展平为独立特征，丢弃了携带关键信号的序列结构。

Method: RNN Maxent将Maxent的固定特征字典替换为GRU神经网络，通过反向传播进行端到端训练。该方法保留了Maxent的存在点统计基础、背景归一化和概率校准，区别仅在于非线性特征由数据学习而非预先固定。实验中采用50天环境时间序列并设置7天间隔以实现预测行为。

Result: 在沙漠蝗虫栖息地适宜性制图任务中，RNN Maxent相对于标准Maxent在各项指标上均有提升：ROC AUC从0.792提升至0.862（std 0.036），F1从0.590提升至0.671（std 0.056）。

Conclusion: RNN Maxent通过引入可学习的时序非线性特征，在保持Maxent统计框架优势的同时，显著提升了物种分布建模尤其是时序环境数据的预测性能，为具有动态环境耦合特征的物种监测提供了更优的方案。

Abstract: Species Distribution Modelling (SDM) is essential for understanding how environmental conditions shape biodiversity, particularly for destructive pests such as the Desert Locust (Schistocerca gregaria), whose breeding dynamics are tightly coupled to rapidly evolving environmental conditions. Maxent has become the dominant method for presence-only data, but its reliance on a linear combination of hand chosen feature transforms limits its ability to capture the nonlinear, temporal relationships common in ecological monitoring, where covariates such as precipitation, soil moisture, and vegetation indices evolve meaningfully over time. Standard implementations flatten time-series covariates into independent features, discarding sequential structure that carries critical signal. We introduce RNN Maxent, an extension of the Maxent framework that replaces the fixed feature dictionary with a neural network, specifically a Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU), trained end to end via backpropagation. The approach preserves Maxent's presence only statistical foundations, background normalization, and probability calibration, differing only in that the nonlinearity is learned from data rather than fixed in advance. We apply RNN Maxent to map suitable habitat for the Desert Locust using 50 day environmental time series derived from ERA5 Land, MODIS, and Sentinel 3, maintaining a 7 day gap between covariates and presence records to yield forecasting behavior. Compared against standard Maxent, RNN Maxent improves performance across metrics (ROC AUC 0.862 std 0.036 vs. 0.792; F1 0.671 std 0.056 vs. 0.590).

</details>


### [230] [On the Interaction Between Model Compression and Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03604)
*Francesco Corti,Dong Wang,Young D. Kwon,Cecilia Mascolo,Olga Saukh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了结构化压缩如何影响深度神经网络在测试时自适应（TTA）的能力。通过在ResNet-18和ViT-Base上结合多种压缩方法和TTA技术进行实验，发现压缩模型在监督条件下精度保持较高，但TTA性能随压缩程度增加而显著下降，原因是表征多样性减少和结构性约束限制了可恢复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在实际部署中既需要高效（模型压缩）又需要适应分布偏移（测试时自适应），这两方面虽然各自研究充分，但二者之间的交互机制尚不清楚。了解压缩对模型可适应性的影响，是设计兼顾效率与鲁棒性模型的关键前提。

Method: 在ResNet-18和ViT-Base上使用CIFAR-10-C和ImageNet-C基准，系统评估多种结构化压缩方法与标准TTA技术的组合；引入诊断框架从表征表达能力（representational expressivity）和自适应子空间兼容性（adaptation subspace compatibility）两个维度分析压缩对TTA的影响机制。

Result: 实验结果揭示了一个一致的差距：压缩模型在监督自适应下仍保持高精度，但其TTA性能随压缩程度增加而显著退化。这种退化源于压缩导致的表征多样性下降和结构性约束限制了模型自适应的可恢复能力，且该效应明显依赖于具体压缩方法。

Conclusion: 压缩本身并不只影响精度，更会损害模型在分布偏移下的自适应能力。研究强调需要设计能够保留表征多样性和适应灵活性的压缩策略，而非仅以压缩后精度为优化目标。

Abstract: Deep neural networks deployed in the wild must be both efficient and adaptable, requiring model compression and test-time adaptation (TTA). While both are well studied in isolation, their interaction remains poorly understood. We systematically analyze how structured compression affects a model's ability to adapt under distribution shift. Using ResNet-18 and ViT-Base on CIFAR-10-C and ImageNet-C, we evaluate multiple compression methods combined with standard TTA techniques. We introduce a diagnostic framework that examines representational expressivity and adaptation subspace compatibility. Our results reveal a consistent gap: although compressed models retain high accuracy under supervised adaptation, their TTA performance degrades significantly with increasing compression. We show that this stems from reduced representational diversity and structural constraints that limit recoverability. These effects strongly depend on the compression method, highlighting the need to design compression strategies that preserve adaptability.

</details>


### [231] [Local Updates, Global Learning (LUGL): Playing Games with non-incremental Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03660)
*David Milec,Spyridon Samothrakis,Michael Fairbank,Dennis J. N. J. Soemers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LUGL框架，通过将数据收集与模型拟合解耦，使LightGBM等梯度提升树（GBT）能够应用于强化学习（RL）的自博弈训练场景，在完美信息和不完美信息游戏中均达到或超越DQN和DeepCFR的性能，挑战了神经网络在游戏智能体中占据主导地位的传统认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
